## 12. How to reproduce

```sh
git checkout 4e8980ff5cabdf6ca7cfe7addeaa97c927de186b
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
pnpm cli report --network eip155:8453 --since 1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z --until 2026-10-25T08:19:27.000Z
```

Every number above comes from a named SQL query in `src/report/queries.ts`, run with `runQuery(db, name, { since: 0, until: 1792916367000, network: "eip155:8453" })` (plus `min_calls: 30` for top sellers and `window_ms: 120000` for duplicate runs). Chain dust floor: 100 atomic.
